<!-- Micro-interaction Script -->
<script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            // Simple visual feedback for drag and drop sources to simulate interactivity
            const draggables = document.querySelectorAll('.drag-source');
            const dropZones = document.querySelectorAll('.border-dashed');

            draggables.forEach(draggable => {
                draggable.addEventListener('dragstart', () => {
                    draggable.classList.add('opacity-50');
                });
                draggable.addEventListener('dragend', () => {
                    draggable.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                });
            });
            
            // In a real app, dragover and drop events would be implemented here
        });
    </script>

<!-- Micro-interaction Script -->
<script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            // Simple visual feedback for drag and drop sources to simulate interactivity
            const draggables = document.querySelectorAll('.drag-source');
            const dropZones = document.querySelectorAll('.border-dashed');

            draggables.forEach(draggable => {
                draggable.addEventListener('dragstart', () => {
                    draggable.classList.add('opacity-50');
                });
                draggable.addEventListener('dragend', () => {
                    draggable.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                });
            });
            
            // In a real app, dragover and drop events would be implemented here
        });
    </script>

<div class="font-label-caps text-label-caps text-secondary mb-2">C++ Core Guidelines</div>
<h4 class="font-body-sm text-body-sm font-semibold text-primary mb-1">RAII Principle</h4>
<p class="font-code-sm text-code-sm text-on-surface-variant text-[11px] leading-relaxed">Resource Acquisition Is Initialization. Bind the life cycle of a resource to the lifetime of a local object.</p>
